Detailed optimizer idea:
- Precompute FFT size N=next_power_of_two(2*n) (16384).
- Function compute_b(a): rfft, square, irfft, return b length L.
- Function score_from_b(b): 2n*max(b)
- Function grad_smoothed(a,b,beta,M? Use softmax weights over top and maybe all). Compute weights p:
   * If beta is None and M given: select top M by b, weights default softmax with temperature gap? Could set p=1/M for top M? Or exponential with temp=max(b)/10. For better descent, weight active peaks by (b - below)^(power). Simpler: p = np.exp(beta*(b-bmax))/sum over selected all if beta>0. For M, only top M. If beta large, top1. Could use top M and beta_rel = beta*(bmax - min_selected) to spread. This is flexible.
   * For dense β small, p over all L.
   * gradient: if M<=80, loop. For each k with p>1e-12, idx range lo=max(0,k-n+1), hi=min(n-1,k), grad[lo:hi+1] += 2*p*a[k-idx]. But note a indexed by k-idx; for slice we can do grad[lo:hi+1] += 2*p * a[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]? Let's derive: idx array lo..hi; need a[k - idx]. When idx=lo, k-lo; when idx=hi, k-hi. This is descending. Slicing a[k-hi : k-lo+1][::-1]. For speed. If M small okay. If dense, FFT crosscorr.
   * Center gradient: grad -= mean(grad) to keep sum (if using additive). For multiplicative, normalization handles sum but subtracting mean can improve.
- Step update:
   Option A: Projected gradient with Nesterov momentum and backtracking on smoothed objective f_s. Compute cur_f. Propose velocity update:
       velocity = β_mom * velocity + g
       y = a + momentum? But simplex projection with momentum tricky.
   Simpler: multiplicative: a_new = a * exp(-lr * (g - mean g))? We can scale g to unit norm. Let dir = g - mean(g). Step = s. a_new = a * exp(-dir / scale?) 
   Multiplicative gradient often: a_new = a * exp(-η ∇f), normalized. If g has mean subtract, sum unaffected before normalize. Use η adaptive.
   Because a_i near 1/n, gradient components maybe O(1/n). We can normalize direction: dir = g/norm_g; step length in log space: a_new ∝ a_i exp(-eta * (g_i / g_scale)). g_scale maybe RMS of g. Choose eta ~0.1? Need line search.

Projective additive may allow zeros and faster. Simplex projection O(nlogn) but okay. Use FISTA:
   f_smooth = bmax + logsumexp(β(b-bmax))/β.
   gradient g.
   L estimate? Use Lipschitz of f maybe β*Hessian max. Hard. Use backtracking: propose a_next = project(a - η g); if f(a_next) > f(a) + c*η*g@(a_next-a) (Armijo) with c=1e-4, reduce η. Hard guarantee but works.
   Nesterov: y = a + (t_k-1)/(t_{k+1}) prev; project(y - η g_y); t update. Might improve.
But nonconvex; line search maybe.

Multiplicative easier line search: no projection. Let dir = g (or centered). Let step = α / ||dir||. Initialize parameters. For it in range(trials): a_try = a * np.exp(-step * dir) then normalize; if finite and score/smooth < f_cur + tol accept, update step*increase; break; step*=0.5. If none, maybe try step*0.1. Let's implement gradient function:
```
def _weighted_gradient(a, b, beta, K=0):
    n=len(a); L=len(b)
    bmax=b.max()
    if beta <=0: # top average?
       ...
    if K <=0 or K > min(L, max(64, int(n//16)))? # dense
       # compute p over all L
       # use beta maybe large? But stable
       x = beta*(b-bmax)
       # if min(x) < -745, exp ok underflow; but sum huge? max exp 1.
       e = np.exp(x)
       s=e.sum()
       if s ==0: p=uniform top1
       else p=e/s
       # check if p too dense? cross-corr FFT
       A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
       P=np.fft.rfft(p,N) ? p is real length L; use rfft returns N/2+1. For cross-corr need circular with N. Using rfft: h = irfft(P * conj(A), N)[:n]. Is that correct with rfft? Yes if P is rfft of p zero padded to N. P*conj(A) -> irfft. Need N even. 
       grad = 2*real(h)
    else:
       idx = argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
       xb= beta*(b[idx]-bmax)
       e=exp(xb); p=e/e.sum()
       grad=zeros(n)
       # for k,wt in idx,p:
       for k,w in zip(idx,p): if w>1e-12:
          lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
          grad[lo:hi+1] += 2*w*a[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
       # Could this be faster for K<20.
```

```
def _log_perturb(a, rng, scale=0.2):
    n=len(a)
    y=a.copy()
    # low freq components
    for _ in range(8):
       f=rng.integers(1, max(2,n//20))
       phase=rng.uniform(0,2π)
       amp=rng.uniform(-1,1)*scale
       y *= np.exp(amp*np.cos(2π f i/n+phase))
    clip min? If exp may drop; if min too small, add epsilon
    y=np.maximum(y, 1e-12)
    y/=y.sum(); return y
```
Also random independent log noise with small scale after smoothing: `y *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,scale,n))`.

```
def _normalize_nonnegative(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=1e-12: a=np.ones(len(a))/len(a); s=1.0
    else: a=a/s
    return a
```

Objective `_objective(z)`:
```

A,b=_conv_and_fft(a)
L=st['L']; beta=st['beta']; K=st['K']
bmax=float(np.max(b))
# weights
if K <=0:
   idx_all=np.arange(L)? But p all. For memory okay.
   x=beta*(b-bmax)
   # To avoid overflow in exp if beta*bmax? subtract max, x<=0, min may be -inf; fine.
   e=np.exp(x)
   s=e.sum()
   if not finite or s==0:
      p=np.zeros(L); p[int(np.argmax(b))]=1.0; s=1.0
   else: p=e/s
   f_extra=np.log(s)/beta
   use_direct=False
   idx_top=None
else:
   K=min(K,L)
   idx_top=np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
   xb=beta*(b[idx_top]-bmax)
   e=np.exp(xb)
   s=e.sum()
   if not finite or s==0:
      p=np.zeros(L); p[int(np.argmax(b))]=1.0; s=1.0
   else: p_idx=e/s; p=np.zeros(L); p[idx_top]=p_idx
   f_extra=np.log(s)/beta
   use_direct=(K <= 16)
if beta==0? we won't.
if use_direct:
   grad_b=np.zeros(n)
   for t in idx_top:
       w=float(p[t])
       if w <=1e-14: continue
       k=int(t)
       lo=max(0, k-n+1); hi=min(n-1, k)
       # vector from a[k-hi] down to a[k-lo]
       grad_b[lo:hi+1] += (2.0*w) * a[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
else:
   P=np.fft.rfft(p, N)
   h=np.fft.irfft(P*np.conj(A), n=N)[:n]
   grad_b=2.0*h.real
# Score
score=2.0*n*(bmax+f_extra)
if not finite: score=np.inf; grad zeros? return
g=2.0*n*grad_b
# Ensure finite
if not all(np.isfinite(g)): g=np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
dot=np.dot(a,g)
gz=a[z_idx]*(g[z_idx]-dot)
gz=np.nan_to_num(gz, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
# exact update
exact=2.0*n*bmax
if exact < st['best_score'] - 1e-12:
    st['best_score']=exact
    st['best_a']=a.copy()
    # print maybe
return float(score), gz
```

```
def _make_two_level(n, eps):
    a=np.ones(n)
    a[:n//2]=1+eps
    a[n//2:]=1-eps
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    return _normalize_nonneg(a)
```

Smooth anti with cos:
```
def _make_cos_profile(n, freq=1, eps=0.6, phase=0.0, quad=0.0):
    i=np.arange(n)
    x=i/float(n-1) if n>1 else 0.0
    a=1.0 + eps*np.cos(np.pi*x + phase) + quad*x*(1-x)
    a=np.maximum(a,1e-12)
    return _normalize_nonneg(a)
```

```
def _make_tanh_anti(n, k=3.0, eps=0.8):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    a=1.0 + eps*np.tanh(k*(0.5-x))
    a=np.maximum(a,1e-12)
    return _normalize_nonneg(a)
```

Polynomial anti:
```
def _make_poly_anti(n, alpha, beta2, gamma3):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    t=2*x-1
    a=1 + alpha*t + beta2*(t*t -1/3) + gamma3*(t*t*t - 0.2*t)
    a=np.maximum(a,1e-12); return normalize
```
Use random coefficients: alpha 0.3..0.8, beta2 ±0.3, gamma3 ±0.3.

Log perturb:
```
def _log_perturb(a, rng, fcount=8, scale=0.5):
    n=len(a); i=np.arange(n)
    logs=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
    for j in range(fcount):
       f=int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n//30)))
       amp=scale*rng.uniform(-1,1)/(j+1)
       phase=float(rng.uniform(0, 2*np.pi))
       logs += amp * np.cos(2*np.pi*f*i/n + phase)
    # clip logs to avoid extremes
    logs = np.clip(logs, np.log(1e-12), np.log(100.0/n))
    ans=np.exp(logs)
    ans=np.maximum(ans,1e-12)
    return _normalize_nonneg(ans)
```
`np.log(100/n)` for max value maybe 100 times mean; enough dynamic. If a has larger values, clip may reduce; okay. If all values near 0? a mean 1/n. 100/n max mean 100x. Good.

Now, code structure with all top-level. Let's draft in analysis.

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy import optimize as _opt

_G = {'n':0,'L':0,'N':1,'base':0,'z_idx':None,'beta':1.0,'K':0,'best_a':None,'best_score':np.inf,'last_print':0.0,'last_print_score':-np.inf, 'bounds':None}

def _next_power_of_two(m):
    p=1
    while p<m: p <<=1
    return p

def _clean_normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size==0: return np.array([1.0])
    a=a.reshape(-1)
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=1e-15:
       a=np.ones_like(a)/a.size
    else:
       a=a/s
    return a

def _conv_and_fft(a):
    N=_G['N']; L=_G['N']? no L
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    b=np.fft.irfft(A*A, n=N)[:_G['L']]
    if b.size:
       mn=float(b.min())
       if mn < -1e-12:
          b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    return A,b

def _score_a(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15: return np.inf
    if not np.isclose(s,1.0,rtol=1e-12,atol=1e-15):
       a=a/s
    else:
       a=a.copy()
    _,b=_conv_and_fft(a)
    return float(2.0*_G['n']*float(b.max()))
```
For b can be negative due rounding; max nonnegative.

`_set_base`:
```
def _set_base_from_a(a):
    n=_G['n']; logs=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); med=float(np.median(logs)); base=int(np.argmin(np.abs(logs-med))); _G['base']=base
    idx=np.arange(n); idx=idx[idx != base]; _G['z_idx']=idx.astype(np.int64)
    if _G['bounds'] is None or len(_G['bounds']) != n-1:
        _G['bounds']=[(-50.0,50.0)]*(n-1)
```
If n==1, idx len0; bounds len0. L-BFGS with 0 vars? Avoid if n==1. But n large.

`_a_to_z`:
```
def _a_to_z(a):
    idx=_G['z_idx']; base_val=max(float(a[_G['base']]),1e-300)
    z=np.log(np.maximum(a[idx],1e-300)/base_val)
    return np.clip(z,-50.0,50.0)
```

`_z_to_a`:
```
def _z_to_a(z):
    n=_G['n']; y=np.zeros(n); y[_G['z_idx']]=z; y[_G['base']]=0.0
    # y already base 0, z clipped. To avoid under/overflow, subtract max.
    m=float(y.max())
    if np.isfinite(m): y = y - m
    a=np.exp(y)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <=1e-300:
       a=np.ones(n)/n
    else: a = a/s
    return a
```
If m large 50, y-m in [-100,0], exp(-100) underflows to 3e-44 not issue. Could be -100, fine.

`_objective(z)`:
Need access K/beta. Use local variables.
```
def _objective(z):
    st=_G; n=st['n']; N=st['N']; L=st['L']; beta=float(st['beta']); K=int(st['K'])
    a=_z_to_a(z)
    A,b=_conv_and_fft(a)
    bmax=float(np.max(b))
    if K <=0:
        x=beta*(b-bmax)
        # use np.exp with where x < -745 to 0? np.exp handles underflow.
        e=np.exp(x)
        s=sum(e)
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <=0:
            idx_top=np.array([int(np.argmax(b))]); p_idx=np.array([1.0]); log_s=0.0
        else:
            # for h need p full
            p=e/s; log_s=np.log(s); use_direct=False; idx_top=None
    else:
        K=min(K,L)
        idx_top=np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
        xb=beta*(b[idx_top]-bmax)
        e=np.exp(xb)
        s=float(e.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <=0:
            idx_top=np.array([int(np.argmax(b))]); e=np.array([1.0]); s=1.0
        p_idx=e/s; log_s=np.log(s)
    f_b=bmax + log_s/beta
    score=2.0*n*f_b
    if K <=0 or K > 16:
       # For K=0, p exists; for K>16 p full? Need define p full.
       if K <=0:
          p_full = p  # from all
       else:
          p_full=np.zeros(L); p_full[idx_top]=p_idx
       P=np.fft.rfft(p_full, N)
       h=np.fft.irfft(P*np.conj(A), n=N)[:n]
       grad_b=2.0*h.real
    else:
       # direct for K>0 and <=16 (in K<=0 branch not)
       grad_b=np.zeros(n)
       for j,k in enumerate(idx_top):
          w=float(p_idx[j])
          if w <=1e-16: continue
          kk=int(k)
          lo=max(0, kk-n+1); hi=min(n-1, kk)
          grad_b[lo:hi+1] += 2.0*w*a[kk-hi:kk-lo+1][::-1]
    g=2.0*n*grad_b
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
       g=np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    dot=float(np.dot(a,g))
    idx=st['z_idx']
    gz=a[idx]*(g[idx] - dot)
    gz=np.nan_to_num(gz, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    exact=2.0*n*bmax
    if exact < st['best_score'] - 1e-12:
       st['best_score']=float(exact)
       st['best_a']=a.copy()
       now=time.monotonic()
       if st['best_score'] < st['last_print_score'] - 1e-6 or now - st['last_print_time'] > 15.0:
           print(f"    obj grad best: {st['best_score']:.7f}")
           st['last_print_score']=st['best_score']; st['last_print_time']=now
    if not np.isfinite(score):
       score=1e100
       gz=np.zeros_like(gz)
    return float(score), gz
```
Need in K<=0 branch, for K>16 gradient FFT uses p full. But if K<=0 and L large, p full ok. If K>0 and K<=16 direct, no p full. For K>16, p full. Good.

Now `_run_lbfgs`:
```
def _run_lbfgs(start_a, limit, schedule, maxiter=80, gtol=1e-7):
    a=_clean_normalize(start_a)
    cur_score=_score_a(a)
    # if global not set, set?
    if cur_score < _G['best_score']:
       _G['best_score']=cur_score; _G['best_a']=a.copy()
    # Set base, beta before first minimize? We'll inside loop.
    while time.monotonic() < limit:
        cycle_improved=False
        for beta,K in schedule:
            if time.monotonic() >= limit: break
            _set_base_from_a(a)
            _G['beta']=float(beta); _G['K']=int(K)
            z0=_a_to_z(a)
            # If z0 contains NaN, skip
            options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'maxfn': int(max(100, 20*maxiter)), 'gtol': float(gtol), 'ftol': 1e-10, 'maxls': 50, 'disp': False}
            try:
                res=_opt.minimize(_objective, z0, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=_G['bounds'], options=options)
            except Exception as exc:
                print("  L-BFGS-B error:", exc)
                break
            if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x') and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
                a_try=_z_to_a(res.x)
                sc_try=_score_a(a_try)
                if sc_try < cur_score - 1e-12:
                    a=a_try.copy(); cur_score=sc_try; cycle_improved=True
                    print(f"  local improve: {cur_score:.7f}")
            # If global best became better than current, use it
            if _G['best_score'] < cur_score - 1e-12 and _G['best_a'] is not None:
                a=_G['best_a'].copy(); cur_score=_G['best_score']
        if not cycle_improved:
            # maybe try a few times with smaller maxiter high beta? If schedule includes high; break to avoid looping
            # If time not too close, perturbed global best? we can just break.
            break
    if cur_score < _G['best_score']:
       _G['best_score']=cur_score; _G['best_a']=a.copy()
    return a, cur_score
```
This loops whole schedule until no improvement. But low beta first each cycle; if high beta already at local and low beta no improvement, break. Good. But if there is improvement, cycles repeat low beta again; may be unnecessary. Could use schedule as phases not cycles: run once, if time remains continue high only. ```

```
def _lbfgs_one(a, beta, K, maxiter, gtol):
    a=_clean_normalize(a)

       try=_z_to_a(res.x); sc_try=_score_a(try)
       if sc_try < _score_a(a) -1e-12: return try, sc_try
    # if global improved, return global?

    return a, _score_a(a)
```

```
    a=_clean_normalize(start_a); cur=_score_a(a); update global
    for beta,K in schedule:
       if time.monotonic()>=limit: break
       a2, sc2 = _lbfgs_one(a,beta,K,maxiter,gtol)
       if sc2 < cur -1e-12:
           a=a2; cur=sc2; print...
       else:
           # if global improved use it
           if _G['best_score'] < cur:
              a=_G['best_a'].copy(); cur=_G['best_score']
    # polish tail

       improved=False
       for beta,K in tail:
           if time.monotonic()>=limit: break

           if sc2 < cur -1e-12:
              a=a2; cur=sc2; improved=True
           else:
              if _G['best_score'] < cur: a=global; cur=global
       if not improved: break
    update global
    return a,cur
```

Before final, let's reason through objective gradient factor one more time with a simple case to ensure no missing 2.
Suppose n=1, a=[1], b=[1]. β any K=1, exact score=2*1*1=2. In `_objective`: a=[1], b=[1], bmax1, p=[1], direct K=1 (<=16) grad_b[0] += 2*1*a[0]=2. g=2*n*grad_b=4. dot=a*g=4. z_idx empty. okay. L-BFGS cannot change. Score objective: f_b=1+log1/beta=1; score=2. Correct. For n=2, a=[0.5,0.5], b=[0.25,0.5,0.25], exact=2*2*0.5=2. If top K includes center p=1, grad_b for k=1: idx 0,1: grad[0]=2*a[1]=1; grad[1]=2*a[0]=1. g=4? n=2 => g=[2,2]. dot=a*g=2. gz for variable idx maybe a0*(g0-2)=0.5*(0)=0. So if a uniform, gradient zero? But score 2; is uniform a local critical? Maybe due symmetry? Actually a uniform has b triangular; moving from [0.5,0.5] to [0.6,0.4] changes b: [0.36,0.48,0.16], max from0.5 to0.36 score 7.2 worse; to [0.9,0.1]: max 0.81 score3.24 worse; so uniform local min?
```
def _lbfgs_one(a, beta, K, maxiter, gtol):
    a=_clean_normalize(a)
    cur=_score_a(a)
    if cur < _G['best_score']:
        _G['best_score']=cur; _G['best_a']=a.copy()
    _set_base_from_a(a)
    _G['beta']=float(beta)
    _G['K']=int(K)
    z0=_a_to_z(a)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z0)): return a,cur
    options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'maxfn': int(max(100, 25*maxiter)), 'gtol': float(gtol), 'ftol': 1e-10, 'maxls': 30, 'disp': False}
    try:
       res=_opt.minimize(_objective,z0,jac=True,method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=_G['bounds'],options=options)
    except Exception as exc:
       print("  minimize exception:", exc)
       return a,cur
    if hasattr(res,'x'):
       x=res.x
       if np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
          a2=_z_to_a(x)
          s2=_score_a(a2)
          if s2 < cur - 1e-12:
             if s2 < _G['best_score']:
                _G['best_score']=s2; _G['best_a']=a2.copy()
             return a2,s2
    if _G['best_score'] < cur and _G['best_a'] is not None:
       return _G['best_a'].copy(), _G['best_score']
    return a,cur
```

In `_make_mseq_anti`, use `period=8191`. If n maybe < half? n large. For safety if n<=1.

```
def _make_mseq_anti(n, shift, eps):
    if n == 0: return np.array([1.0])
    m=13; period=(1<<m)-1; mask=period; state=period
    bits=[0]*period
    for i in range(period):
       bits[i]=state & 1
       fb=((state>>12) ^ (state>>3)) & 1
       state=((state<<1) & mask) ^ fb
    half=n//2
    s=np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    for i in range(half):
       bit=bits[(i+shift)%period]
       val=1.0 if bit else -1.0
       s[i]=val
       s[n-1-i]=-val
    if n%2:
       s[half]=1.0
    a=1.0 + eps*s
    return _clean_normalize(a)
```

```
        with np.errstate(under='ignore', over='ignore', invalid='ignore'):
```

`_run_lbfgs`: Use schedule list of tuples; for each. `tail = schedule[-3:]`. If schedule empty return.

```
def _run_lbfgs(start_a, limit, schedule, maxiter=60, gtol=1e-7):
    a = _clean_normalize(start_a)
    cur = _score_a(a)
    if cur < _G['best_score']:
       _G['best_score']=cur; _G['best_a']=a.copy()
    for beta,K in schedule:
       if time.monotonic() >= limit: break
       a2,s2 = _lbfgs_one(a,beta,K,maxiter,gtol)
       if s2 < cur - 1e-12:
          a=a2; cur=s2
          print(f"  improve to {cur:.7f}")
       else:
          if _G['best_score'] < cur and _G['best_a'] is not None:
              a=_G['best_a'].copy(); cur=_G['best_score']
    tail=schedule[-3:]
    while time.monotonic() + 5.0 < limit and len(tail):
       improved=False
       for beta,K in tail:
          if time.monotonic() >= limit: break
          a2,s2=_lbfgs_one(a,beta,K,maxiter,gtol)
          if s2 < cur - 1e-12:
             a=a2; cur=s2; improved=True
          else:
             if _G['best_score'] < cur and _G['best_a'] is not None:
                a=_G['best_a'].copy(); cur=_G['best_score']
       if not improved:
          break
    if cur < _G['best_score']:
       _G['best_score']=cur; _G['best_a']=a.copy()
    return a,cur
```
If schedule large and tail repeated, if no improvement break. Good.

Now, final code. Ensure no nested functions/lambdas. List comprehensions okay? The rule "Don't use any lambda functions" only lambda. List comprehensions okay. But to be safe, avoid in critical. Use loops for starts? List comprehensions for `result` okay. No lambdas.

Let's write final code in code block.

Double-check `_make_mseq_anti`: If `shift` >= period? use `shift = int(shift) % period`. If n small, half maybe0; s empty? For n=1, s[0]=1? We can handle: if n==1 return [1]. Good.

In `_make_mseq_anti`, after `for i in range(half)`, set mirrored; if n odd center assigned. If n even, all assigned. Good.

Potential correct LFSR: Even if not m-sequence, okay. Use state initial mask (8191). The feedback `(state >> 12) ^ (state >> 3)`; after shift, state may become 0? If state zero, feedback zero and fails. Initial not zero; primitive polynomial shouldn't hit zero. Good.

Now, final check on `_objective` p_full branch for use_all and K>16: For use_all, `p_full` defined. For K>16, p_full constructed. In code, if use_all, `K` from state maybe 0 or >=L; `K >16` false if 0; condition `use_all or K > 16` true due use_all. Good. If K>16 and use_all false, p_full constructed. Good.

In direct branch, `use_all or K > 16` false implies not use_all and K<=16. K>0. p_idx exists. Good.

Now, in `_score_a`, we use `_conv_and_fft(a)` which sets b nonnegative. It normalizes a: If s not close to1, `a = a / s`, else `a = a.copy()`. If s=1 but a maybe integer? After division, copy. Good.

Potential issue: `_G['n']` might not match length a if parent length different. `_score_a` uses `_G['n']` factor; if a length differs, could fail. In propose n set from parent. `_clean_normalize` keeps same. Good.

Now, final answer only code. Ensure fenced. Use print limited.

One more thought: The evaluate_sequence clips sequence to [0,1000] and sum. Our a values ~1e-4. Sum=1. It calculates `b_sequence = np.convolve(sequence, sequence)` using Python floats. Our internal FFT may have slightly different max. final verify. If internal best but actual evaluate slightly worse due rounding? negligible. Now, final code.

```python
"""
Local L-BFGS-B search for non-negative step-function heights.

The objective is scale invariant, so the optimizer works on sum-one vectors.
For a chosen temperature beta, it minimizes a top-K log-sum-exp surrogate
of the convolution peak. Gradients are computed by FFT.  Multiple diverse
starts and periodic perturbations are used to avoid local minima.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy import optimize as _opt

_G = {
    "n": 0,
    "L": 0,
    "N": 1,
    "base": 0,
    "z_idx": None,
    "beta": 1.0,
    "K": 0,
    "best_a": None,
    "best_score": np.inf,
    "bounds": None,
    "last_print_time": 0.0,
    "last_print_score": np.inf,
}


def _next_power_of_two(m):
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def _clean_normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a.reshape(-1)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        a = np.ones_like(a, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(a.sum())
    a = a / s
    return a


def _conv_and_fft(a):
    N = _G["N"]
    L = _G["L"]
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    b = np.fft.irfft(A * A, n=N)[:L]
    if b.size:
        mn = float(b.min())
        if mn < -1e-12:
            b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    return A, b


def _score_a(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = _G["n"]
    if a.size != n:
        a = _clean_normalize(a)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.inf
    if abs(s - 1.0) > 1e-12:
        a = a / s
    else:
        a = a.copy()
    _, b = _conv_and_fft(a)
    return float(2.0 * n * float(b.max()))


def _set_base_from_a(a):
    n = _G["n"]
    logs = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    med = float(np.median(logs))
    base = int(np.argmin(np.abs(logs - med)))
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    idx = idx[idx != base]
    _G["base"] = base
    _G["z_idx"] = idx
    if _G["bounds"] is None or len(_G["bounds"]) != n - 1:
        _G["bounds"] = [(-70.0, 70.0)] * max(1, n - 1)


def _a_to_z(a):
    idx = _G["z_idx"]
    base_val = max(float(a[_G["base"]]), 1e-300)
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a[idx], 1e-300) / base_val)
    return np.clip(z, -70.0, 70.0)


def _z_to_a(z):
    n = _G["n"]
    y = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    y[_G["z_idx"]] = z
    y[_G["base"]] = 0.0
    m = float(y.max())
    if np.isfinite(m):
        y = y - m
    a = np.exp(y)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        a = a / s
    return a


def _objective(z):
    st = _G
    n = st["n"]
    N = st["N"]
    L = st["L"]
    beta = float(st["beta"])
    K = int(st["K"])

    a = _z_to_a(z)
    A, b = _conv_and_fft(a)
    bmax = float(np.max(b))

    use_all = (K <= 0 or K >= L)
    p_full = None
    idx_top = None
    p_idx = None
    log_s = 0.0

    if use_all:
        x = beta * (b - bmax)
        with np.errstate(under="ignore", over="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
            e = np.exp(x)
        s = float(e.sum())
        if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
            p_full = e / s
            log_s = float(np.log(s))
        else:
            idxm = int(np.argmax(b))
            p_full = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            p_full[idxm] = 1.0
            log_s = 0.0
    else:
        K = max(1, min(K, L))
        idx_top = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
        xb = beta * (b[idx_top] - bmax)
        with np.errstate(under="ignore", over="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
            e = np.exp(xb)
        s = float(e.sum())
        if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
            p_idx = e / s
            log_s = float(np.log(s))
        else:
            idx_top = np.array([int(np.argmax(b))], dtype=np.int64)
            p_idx = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
            log_s = 0.0

    f_b = bmax + log_s / beta
    score = 2.0 * n * f_b

    if use_all or K > 16:
        if p_full is None:
            p_full = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            p_full[idx_top] = p_idx
        P = np.fft.rfft(p_full, N)
        h = np.fft.irfft(P * np.conj(A), n=N)[:n]
        grad_b = 2.0 * h.real
    else:
        grad_b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for j in range(len(idx_top)):
            w = float(p_idx[j])
            if w <= 1e-16:
                continue
            kk = int(idx_top[j])
            lo = max(0, kk - n + 1)
            hi = min(n - 1, kk)
            grad_b[lo:hi + 1] += 2.0 * w * a[kk - hi:kk - lo + 1][::-1]

    g = 2.0 * n * grad_b
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

    dot = float(np.dot(a, g))
    idx = st["z_idx"]
    gz = a[idx] * (g[idx] - dot)
    gz = np.nan_to_num(gz, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

    exact = 2.0 * n * bmax
    if exact < st["best_score"] - 1e-12:
        st["best_score"] = float(exact)
        st["best_a"] = a.copy()
        now = time.monotonic()
        if st["best_score"] < st["last_print_score"] - 1e-6 or (now - st["last_print_time"] > 15.0):
            print(f"    L-BFGS pass found: {st['best_score']:.7f}")
            st["last_print_score"] = st["best_score"]
            st["last_print_time"] = now

    if not np.isfinite(score):
        score = 1e100
        gz = np.zeros_like(gz)

    return float(score), gz


def _lbfgs_one(a, beta, K, maxiter, gtol):
    a = _clean_normalize(a)
    cur = _score_a(a)
    if cur < _G["best_score"]:
        _G["best_score"] = cur
        _G["best_a"] = a.copy()

    _set_base_from_a(a)
    _G["beta"] = float(beta)
    _G["K"] = int(K)
    z0 = _a_to_z(a)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z0)):
        return a, cur

    options = {
        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
        "maxfn": int(max(100, 25 * maxiter)),
        "gtol": float(gtol),
        "ftol": 1e-10,
        "maxls": 30,
        "disp": False,
    }

    try:
        res = _opt.minimize(
            _objective,
            z0,
            jac=True,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            bounds=_G["bounds"],
            options=options,
        )
    except Exception as exc:
        print("  L-BFGS-B exception:", repr(exc))
        return a, cur

    if hasattr(res, "x") and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
        x = res.x
        if np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            a2 = _z_to_a(x)
            s2 = _score_a(a2)
            if s2 < cur - 1e-12:
                if s2 < _G["best_score"]:
                    _G["best_score"] = s2
                    _G["best_a"] = a2.copy()
                return a2, s2

    if _G["best_score"] < cur and _G["best_a"] is not None:
        return _G["best_a"].copy(), _G["best_score"]

    return a, cur


def _run_lbfgs(start_a, limit, schedule, maxiter=60, gtol=1e-7):
    a = _clean_normalize(start_a)
    cur = _score_a(a)
    if cur < _G["best_score"]:
        _G["best_score"] = cur
        _G["best_a"] = a.copy()

    for beta, K in schedule:
        if time.monotonic() >= limit:
            break
        a2, s2 = _lbfgs_one(a, beta, K, maxiter, gtol)
        if s2 < cur - 1e-12:
            a = a2
            cur = s2
            print(f"  phase improve: {cur:.7f}")
        else:
            if _G["best_score"] < cur and _G["best_a"] is not None:
                a = _G["best_a"].copy()
                cur = _G["best_score"]

    tail = schedule[-3:]
    while time.monotonic() + 5.0 < limit and len(tail) > 0:
        improved = False
        for beta, K in tail:
            if time.monotonic() >= limit:
                break
            a2, s2 = _lbfgs_one(a, beta, K, maxiter, gtol)
            if s2 < cur - 1e-12:
                a = a2
                cur = s2
                improved = True
            else:
                if _G["best_score"] < cur and _G["best_a"] is not None:
                    a = _G["best_a"].copy()
                    cur = _G["best_score"]
        if not improved:
            break

    if cur < _G["best_score"]:
        _G["best_score"] = cur
        _G["best_a"] = a.copy()

    return a, cur


def _log_perturb(a, rng, scale=0.25, fcount=8):
    a = _clean_normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    logs = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))

    for j in range(fcount):
        f = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n // 30)))
        amp = float(scale * rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0) / float(j + 1))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        logs += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * i / float(n) + phase)

    lo = np.log(1e-12)
    hi = np.log(100.0 / float(max(1, n)))
    logs = np.clip(logs, lo, hi)
    out = np.exp(logs)
    out = np.maximum(out, 1e-300)
    return _clean_normalize(out)


def _make_two_level(n, eps, p=None):
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    if p is None:
        p = n // 2
    p = max(1, min(n - 1, int(p)))
    a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[:p] = 1.0 + eps
    a[p:] = 1.0 - eps
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    return _clean_normalize(a)


def _make_mseq_anti(n, shift, eps):
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)

    m = 13
    period = (1 << m) - 1
    mask = period
    state = period
    bits = np.empty(period, dtype=np.int64)

    for i in range(period):
        bits[i] = state & 1
        fb = ((state >> 12) ^ (state >> 3)) & 1
        state = ((state << 1) & mask) ^ fb

    shift = int(shift) % period
    half = n // 2
    s = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(half):
        bit = int(bits[(i + shift) % period])
        v = 1.0 if bit else -1.0
        s[i] = v
        s[n - 1 - i] = -v
    if n % 2 == 1:
        s[half] = 1.0

    a = 1.0 + eps * s
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    return _clean_normalize(a)


def _add_start(starts, a):
    a = _clean_normalize(a)
    sc = _score_a(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc):
        starts.append((sc, len(starts), a))


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except NameError:
        parent = np.ones(6121, dtype=np.float64)

    parent = np.nan_to_num(parent, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    parent = np.maximum(parent, 0.0)
    if parent.size == 0:
        parent = np.ones(6121, dtype=np.float64)

    n = int(parent.size)
    _G["n"] = n
    _G["L"] = 2 * n - 1
    _G["N"] = _next_power_of_two(2 * n)
    _G["best_a"] = None
    _G["best_score"] = np.inf
    _G["bounds"] = [(-70.0, 70.0)] * max(1, n - 1)

    parent = _clean_normalize(parent)
    parent_score = _score_a(parent)
    _G["best_a"] = parent.copy()
    _G["best_score"] = parent_score
    _G["last_print_score"] = parent_score
    _G["last_print_time"] = time.monotonic()

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, float(budget_s) - 15.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    starts = []
    _add_start(starts, parent)

    print(f"initial parent score: {parent_score:.7f}, n={n}, N={_G['N']}")

    # Smooth perturbations of the parent.
    for scale in (0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.55):
        _add_start(starts, _log_perturb(parent, rng, scale=scale, fcount=8))

    # Simple analytic profiles.
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    t = 2.0 * x - 1.0

    for eps in (0.25, 0.50, 0.75, 0.95):
        _add_start(starts, _clean_normalize(1.0 + eps * t))

    for eps in (0.40, 0.70, 0.95):
        a = 1.0 + eps * np.cos(np.pi * x)
        _add_start(starts, _clean_normalize(a))

    for eps in (0.40, 0.75):
        a = 1.0 + eps * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)
        _add_start(starts, _clean_normalize(a))

    for k in (2.0, 5.0, 9.0):
        a = 1.0 + 0.85 * np.tanh(k * (0.5 - x))
        _add_start(starts, _clean_normalize(a))

    for _ in range(4):
        alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.10, 0.95))
        beta2 = float(rng.uniform(-0.40, 0.40))
        gamma3 = float(rng.uniform(-0.40, 0.40))
        a = 1.0 + alpha * t + beta2 * (t * t - 1.0 / 3.0) + gamma3 * (t * t * t - 0.2 * t)
        _add_start(starts, _clean_normalize(a))

    # A quick two-level sweep; often produces a very useful basin.
    if n > 32:
        best_tl_a = None
        best_tl_sc = np.inf
        step = max(1, n // 70)
        for p in range(max(2, step), n - 1, step):
            for eps in (0.25, 0.55, 0.80, 0.95):
                a = _make_two_level(n, eps, p)
                sc = _score_a(a)
                if sc < best_tl_sc:
                    best_tl_sc = sc
                    best_tl_a = a
        if best_tl_a is not None:
            _add_start(starts, best_tl_a)

    # M-sequence-like anti-palindromic starts.
    for sh in (0, 1500, 3500, 5500):
        for eps in (1.0, 0.90, 0.75):
            _add_start(starts, _make_mseq_anti(n, sh, eps))

    starts.sort()

    print(f"number of candidates: {len(starts)}")
    print("best 5 starts:", ", ".join(f"{s:.6f}" for s, _, _ in starts[:5]))

    FULL_SCHEDULE = [
        (200, 256),
        (600, 0),
        (2000, 128),
        (8000, 64),
        (30000, 32),
        (100000, 16),
        (300000, 8),
    ]
    POLISH_SCHEDULE = [
        (30000, 32),
        (100000, 16),
        (300000, 8),
        (800000, 4),
    ]

    # Reserve final polish time.
    total = max(1.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    reserve_time = max(180.0, 0.22 * total)
    stage_limit = deadline - reserve_time

    # First candidate gets a generous budget.
    now = time.monotonic()
    if len(starts) > 0 and now < stage_limit:
        first_limit = min(stage_limit, now + 0.38 * max(0.0, stage_limit - now))
        if first_limit > now + 30.0:
            _run_lbfgs(starts[0][2], first_limit, FULL_SCHEDULE, maxiter=90, gtol=1e-8)

    # Then try the remaining candidates, still keeping a final polish reserve.
    base_alloc = max(45.0, 0.07 * total)
    for i in range(1, len(starts)):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= stage_limit - 30.0:
            break
        if i < 4:
            alloc = base_alloc
        elif i < 10:
            alloc = 0.65 * base_alloc
        else:
            alloc = 0.35 * base_alloc
        limit = min(stage_limit, now + alloc)
        if limit <= now + 20.0:
            break
        _run_lbfgs(starts[i][2], limit, FULL_SCHEDULE, maxiter=60, gtol=1e-7)

    # Use any leftover stage time with perturbations of the current best.
    now = time.monotonic()
    if len(starts) > 0 and _G["best_a"] is not None:
        while now < stage_limit - 60.0:
            limit = min(stage_limit, now + 120.0)
            if limit <= now + 30.0:
                break
            pert = _log_perturb(_G["best_a"], rng, scale=0.25, fcount=10)
            _run_lbfgs(pert, limit, FULL_SCHEDULE, maxiter=60, gtol=1e-7)
            now = time.monotonic()

    # Final polishing and escape attempts.
    a_final = _G["best_a"].copy() if _G["best_a"] is not None else parent.copy()
    sc_final = _score_a(a_final)
    if sc_final < _G["best_score"]:
        _G["best_score"] = sc_final
        _G["best_a"] = a_final.copy()

    stall = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 20.0:
        improved = False
        for beta, K in POLISH_SCHEDULE:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 20.0:
                break
            a2, s2 = _lbfgs_one(a_final, beta, K, 100, 1e-9)
            if s2 < sc_final - 1e-12:
                a_final = a2
                sc_final = s2
                improved = True

        if _G["best_score"] < sc_final:
            a_final = _G["best_a"].copy()
            sc_final = _G["best_score"]
        else:
            if sc_final < _G["best_score"]:
                _G["best_score"] = sc_final
                _G["best_a"] = a_final.copy()

        if not improved:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 150.0:
                break
            stall += 1
            scale = min(0.65, 0.15 + 0.08 * stall)
            a_final = _log_perturb(a_final, rng, scale=scale, fcount=12)
            sc_final = _score_a(a_final)
            if sc_final < _G["best_score"]:
                _G["best_score"] = sc_final
                _G["best_a"] = a_final.copy()

    # Synchronize final best with global best.
    if _G["best_a"] is None:
        best = parent
        final_internal = parent_score
    else:
        best = _clean_normalize(_G["best_a"])
        final_internal = _score_a(best)
        if final_internal < _G["best_score"]:
            _G["best_score"] = final_internal
            _G["best_a"] = best.copy()

    result = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, v))) for v in best]
    try:
        final_eval = float(evaluate_sequence(result))
    except Exception:
        final_eval = final_internal

    print(f"FINAL internal score: {final_internal:.7f}")
    print(f"FINAL evaluator score: {final_eval:.7f}")
    print(f"elapsed: {time.monotonic() - t0:.1f}s")
    return result
```
